## `AGO-6399-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** PTP-IEEE1588-Clock Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Time Protocol Sub-Microsecond Clock Sync
- **Intro:** PTP-IEEE1588-Clock Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Time Protocol Sub-Microsecond Clock Sync — Pre-Calibration Safety and Isolation Check: No live voltage > 30 VAC or > 60 VDC during setup. ESD wrist strap resistance must be between 0.75 MΩ and 1.25 MΩ.
- **Key tolerance:** No live voltage > 30 VAC or > 60 VDC during setup. ESD wrist strap resistance must be between 0.75 MΩ and 1.25 MΩ.
- **Standards:** `IEEE 1588-2019`, `ITU-T G.8275.1 (Phase/time synchronization)`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`, `IEC 61508:2010`
